len(story_odds) >= 3:\n                combined_series = []\n                for o in full_series + story_odds:\n                    if o not in combined_series:\n                        combined_series.append(o)\n            else:\n                combined_series = full_series\n\n            if len(combined_series) < 2:\n                continue\n\n            for i in range(len(combined_series) - 1):\n                for j in range(i + 1, len(combined_series)):\n                    entry, exit = combined_series[i], combined_series[j]\n                    segment = combined_series[i:j+1]\n                    move_dir = \"drift\" if exit > entry else \"steam\"\n                    trade_type = \"lay-to-back\" if move_dir == \"drift\" else \"back-to-lay\"\n                    pattern_key = f\"{move_dir}→OC{i}-OC{j}→{classify_tick_pattern(entry, exit, segment)}→{trade_type}\"\n\n                    runner_trades.append({\n                        \"runner\": runner_name,\n                        \"market_id\": market_id,\n                        \"selection_id\": selection_id,\n                        \"entry\": entry,\n                        \"exit\": {e}\")\n\n    # Summaries\n    from collections import defaultdict\n    pattern_summary = defaultdict(lambda: {\"total\": 0, \"profitable\": 0, \"confidence_sum\": 0.0})\n    for trade in Blueprint Patterns\")\n    try:\n        enriched_patterns = {}\n        for key, summary in pattern_summary.items():\n            start_oc = key.split(\"→\")[1].split(\"-\")[0]\n            phase = map_oc_to_phase(key.split(\"→\")[1])\n            story_id = f\"{start_oc}-{phase}\"\n            enriched_patterns[key] = {\n                \"sequence\": key.split(\"→\"),\n                \"meta\": round(summary[\"confidence_sum\"] / summary[\"total\"], 3) if summary[\"total\"] > 0 else 0.0,\n                    \"story_phase\": phase,\n                    \"oc_band\": key.split(\"→\")[1],\n                    \"story_id\": story_id\n                }\n            }\n        with open(\"known_blueprints.py\", \"w\") as f:\n            f.write(\"KNOWN_BLUEPRINT_PATTERNS = \")\n to structured JSON format\")\n\n    pattern_dict_export = {}\n    for trade in runner_trades:\n        key = trade.get(\"pattern_key\")\n        if key:\n            if key not in {len(pattern_dict_export)} blueprint patterns to {filename}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"❌ Failed to write blueprint signals: {e}\")\n\n    try:\n        with
